# Distribution Comparison Report

3. 🤖 **LLMDetector (gemini-3-pro)** - Direct LLM-based detection (gemini-3-pro)
4. 🤖 **LLMDetector (claude-opus-4-5)** - Direct LLM-based detection (claude-opus-4-5)
5. 🤖 **LLMDetector (deepseek-v3.2)** - Direct LLM-based detection (deepseek-v3.2)
6. 🗺️ **RepologyMapper** - Direct Repology API mapping
7. 🤖 **RosdepCIMapper** - RosdepCI mapping
8. ✅ **RosdepDatabase (Ground Truth)**
